# Third-party notices

PeerParty is MIT-licensed (see [LICENSE](LICENSE)). `peerparty.exe` also contains
the work of the projects below, each under its own licence. The licence texts are
reproduced here, as those licences require of a binary download. A crate offered
under a choice of licences is used under its permissive option (MIT, Apache-2.0 and
the like), and only that option's text is reproduced. Generated from Cargo.lock by
the release tooling; do not edit by hand.

## Code ported or copied from other projects

### Luxon and Luxon Server (`hook/src/photon`)

```text
The Photon server in this folder is a Rust port of parts of Luxon
(https://gitlab.com/luxon_project/Luxon, the protocol library: ENet, GpBinary v1.6 and
v1.8, encryption) and Luxon Server (https://github.com/niansa/LuxonServer, the
name, master and game servers), read at Luxon Server commit 147e5e5 (2026-10-30)
and Luxon commit 809c9ce (2026-10-11). Both are under the BSD 3-Clause License,
reproduced below with each project's copyright line.
```

## Rust crates linked into the program

175 crates: the ones linked into the Windows x64 and x86 builds (Cargo.lock
also lists crates for other platforms and build-time tools, which aren't shipped).
Where several crates share a licence text, they are listed
together above it. `vendor/retour` (retour with local fixes) is among them.
